<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user, communicating primarily in Turkish, has directed the development of a full-stack algorithmic trading platform through a series of phased task orders (GÖREV EMRİ). The overarching goal is to make the system production-ready and provably robust.

Key requirements executed over the project's lifetime include:
- **Core Trading Features (FAZ-C):** Implementing an end-to-end trading flow, from a trade entry panel and screener to live execution on the Binance testnet.
- **UI/UX & Branding:** Integrating a logo, redesigning login pages, implementing optional MFA (TOTP & Email OTP), and creating a persistent branding settings page.
- **Telemetry & Explainability:** Adding admin-facing dashboards and user-facing panels to explain trade/screener logic.
- **Production Hardening (FAZ-0 & FAZ-1):** A series of strict, evidence-based tasks to improve system reliability.
    - **FAZ 0:** Completely remove all SQLite dependencies and fallbacks, enforce a hard dependency on PostgreSQL, and verify data persistence across restarts.
    - **FAZ 1:** Implement and verify a complete PostgreSQL backup and restore mechanism, including automated backups via a backend scheduler.
- **CI/CD & Quality Gates:** Build a comprehensive CI/CD pipeline () to act as a quality gate, including linting (), unit tests, backend contract tests, and frontend performance smoke tests.
- **System Audits:** Perform multiple deep-dive audits to find and fix critical bugs, such as a 500 error on trade approvals and CI/CD pipeline failures.

The current user request is to finalize the backup system by adding AWS S3 as an off-site storage target and making the backup/restore test a mandatory, merge-blocking CI check.

**User's preferred language**: Turkish (Türkçe)

**what currently exists?**
A feature-complete algorithmic trading platform with a React frontend and FastAPI backend. The system has undergone significant hardening. All code related to SQLite has been purged, and the application now relies exclusively on a PostgreSQL database, with runtime guards to prevent accidental fallback. A robust backup and restore mechanism using a backend scheduler has been implemented and verified locally. The CI/CD pipeline in GitHub Actions is highly advanced, performing multi-stage linting, smoke tests, contract tests, and performance baseline checks. All previously identified critical bugs and CI failures have been resolved.

**Code Architecture**


**Key Technical Concepts**
- **Database**: Strictly **PostgreSQL**. All SQLite fallbacks have been removed. The application now uses .
- **Backup/Restore**: Database dumps are handled by  and , orchestrated by shell scripts.
- **Scheduling**: Automated tasks (like backups) are run via an  background task within the FastAPI  event.
- **CI/CD**: A comprehensive GitHub Actions workflow () that acts as a production gate. It includes services (Postgres, Redis), environment variable injection, multi-stage linting (), and multiple test suites (smoke, contract, latency).
- **Configuration Management**: Moving towards externalizing configuration from code, as seen with .
